- **Main goal:** Athletic performance
- **Sport:** American football - tight end / linebacker (TE / LB, FB, box S) · Early off-season
- **Level:** Advanced (L3): 3 years of consistent training, training 4 days a week now
- **Plan type:** 12 weeks (recommended) · Week 1-12
- **Start date:** 11 Jan 2027
- **Training days per week:** 4 · Monday, Tuesday, Thursday, Friday · about 9-12 exercises a session (plus the warm-up and the 2 core exercises)
- Terms and release of liability accepted before this plan was built: client, English
- Health check: answered; nothing in it asked for a doctor first. [Official PAR-Q+ (in English)](https://eparmedx.com/)

## Before you start

- Biceps, Triceps: one exercise of their own on the training days (the template's arm slot), held at its sets. They are not stepped up like the other groups: progress them with weight and reps.
- This template holds about 83% of your sport's weekly jump contacts (box, squat, broad and reactive jumps): the work climbs across the block (not every week). Add a station or a day if you want the full dose.
- Two-leg lifts are about 61 % of your lower-body lifting sets (your sport's guide is about 30 %): the coach's A and B days keep a two-leg squat and a two-leg hinge. If you want more single-leg work, use the single-leg swap of one of them.

## Basic Instruction
> \*Always leave 1 rep in the tank, never work up to failure but get as close as you can.
>
> \*\*Do steady cardio whenever you want, just don't overdo it... I recommend doing it on your aerobic days, max 40 min of steady.
>
> \*\*\*The lower the reps, the higher the weight... Always go for 1-3 RIR (reps in reserve), aka RPE 7+.
>
> \*\*\*\*When there are joint pains, usually going unilateral and/or getting away from fixed grips (barbells, bars) and going to split grips (dumbbells, kettlebells, individual grips) helps take care of some stuff.
>
> \*\*\*\*\*If you have any questions just ask me - IG @BLAZECAMP or TWITTER @BLAZECAMP\_

_This plan starts at 3 in the tank and ends at 1 in the last week: the coach's "1 in the tank" is where you finish, so follow each week's number._

_In this plan your cardio sessions sit on lifting days: do each one as its own session that day, or move it to any free day._

Planned effort across the stairway: weeks 1-6: leave 3 in the tank; weeks 7-9: leave 2 in the tank; weeks 10-11: leave 1-2 in the tank; week 12: leave 1 in the tank. Week 12: one intensifier on Friday: a MYO set on the day's last small-muscle or machine exercise.

How heavy? Each exercise tells you how many reps to leave in the tank: "leave 2 in the tank" means you could do 2 more reps at the end of the set (about RPE 8; 3 left = RPE 7, 1 left = RPE 9). Your plan starts with 3 in the tank, then 2, then 1-2, and in the last week 1 with a few extra-hard sets. Week 1: start a little lighter, with a weight you could lift about 3 more times.

Olympic lifts are for power. Cleans, snatches and their dumbbell and kettlebell versions train power: move the weight as fast as you can with clean technique. Pick a weight you can move explosively on every rep; if it slows down or your technique breaks, it's too heavy. You don't need percentages for these unless you compete in Olympic weightlifting.

Core supersets are optional anywhere: you can pair a core exercise with any exercise to save time.

## How you progress

- Reps and weight follow a rule you apply yourself, not a number this plan promises. Every lift shows a rep range. Example from your plan: Front Squat (barbell), 4 sets of 8-10. When you hit 10 on every set with the planned reps left in the tank and good form, add weight next time and start again at 8. Not there yet? Keep the weight and add a rep where you can.
- Sets follow the stairway: the plan adds sets week by week until each muscle group reaches its final sets in the last week (see the week-by-week table). In a week with the same sets as the week before, you progress with reps or weight.
- Your next block starts a little above where this one started (about 5 % more sets, in whole sets), never above the top number for your level. At the top, the sets stay and you progress with weight and reps.
- This plan is a template you can edit: change the sets of any exercise on any day or week, add or remove exercises, and switch blocks off.

## How to adjust this plan

- Missed most of a week? Repeat that week before you move on. Missed one session? Do it on the next free day or let it go: never squeeze two sessions into one.
- A set felt like a true max (no rep left)? Next time take one set off that exercise, or a little weight.
- Everything feels easy? Add a little weight or a rep first; never jump ahead more than one week.
- Something hurts? First use a listed swap, then cut that exercise's sets by a third to a half, then drop it for the rest of the block. See a professional if it keeps hurting.
- Life got in the way for several weeks (sick, travel, busy)? Stop the block, take the recommended easy week if you want, then build a new block.
- You (or your coach) can switch off these blocks on any day: jumps, throws and speed; power / Olympic lifts; core; conditioning; cardio; accessories. You can also add or remove exercises (more rear-delt work, for example). Use the suggested supersets to save time.
- Switching off the accessories block takes away every direct set for Biceps, Triceps and Calves.
- You (or your coach) can also change the sets of any exercise on any day or week: the plan is a template.
- The core block is optional: you may switch it off or change its exercises on any day. Everything in this plan is adjustable.
- The time shown on each day is an average estimate, for information only: nothing in this plan is cut or changed because of time. Want a shorter day? Use the suggested supersets, or remove what the day lists as easiest to remove.

## Optional add-ons

_Not part of your template. Add any of these if you want; the plan is complete without them._

- **Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue)** — 4-8 × 5-10 s · Extra work that fits your sport or activity.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Split-Step%20Reaction%20Drill%20(split-step%2C%20then%20shuffle%20%2F%20crossover%20%2F%20drop-step%20on%20a%20cue)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Split-Step Reaction Drill (split-step, then shuffle / crossover / drop-step on a cue)`
- **Farmer Carry (bilateral)** — 2-4 × 20-40 m (or 20-40 s) · Do it with your core or conditioning work; it does not count as muscle sets.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Farmer%20Carry%20(bilateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Farmer Carry (bilateral)`

Your plan climbs like a stairway: small steps up to week 6, a bit bigger from week 7, the hardest weeks at the end.

## Week 1 — Getting used to it (small steps)

_Week level: 2 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**Baseline test this week (before you load up)** (Strength, Speed / agility, Jumps)

**Athletic work this week:** 51 jump contacts · 58 ankle-hop contacts · 63 throws · 81 direction changes · 150 m of sprints · 8 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 34% · Receive-Create 1: Land

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_Average time: about 105-130 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, conditioning. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) + Copenhagen Plank (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) + Medball Chest Pass (about 4.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Chin-Up (supinated) + Side (Lateral) Lunge (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Concentration Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): finisher: Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski) · extra jump / throw stations: Lateral Shuffles, Medball Chest Pass, Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine) · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Turkish Get-Ups (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Kettlebell Windmills - kneeling or standing (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Oblique%20Rotation%20(cable%20rotational%20twist)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Press (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Acceleration Starts** — 3 × 5 x 10 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Falling-Start Accelerations (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Acceleration%20Starts) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Acceleration Starts`
2. **Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing)** — 3 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Jump, then land and stick it: hold the landing 2-3 s, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Depth Drop (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Box%20Jump%20(sideways%20onto%20a%20low%20box%2C%20stick%20the%20landing)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing)`
3. **Medball Chest Pass** — 3 × 9 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Overhead Throw (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Chest%20Pass) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Chest Pass`
4. **Lateral Shuffles** — 3 × 9 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Slant Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Flat Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Dig / Out Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lateral%20Shuffles) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lateral Shuffles`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean & Jerk** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hex-Bar Jump Squat (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch)

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 1 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test first: warm up and work up to one top set (3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max). It replaces this lift's top set this week.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - These 2 sets are your back-off sets after the test: about 3 reps in the tank.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Low-Bar Back Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High-Bar Back Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the heavier barbell load) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20Barbell%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)`
4. **Chin-Up (supinated)** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Chin-Up%20(supinated)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Chin-Up (supinated)`
5. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.

### Thursday · A2 - leg press / belt squat + pull
_Average time: about 90-110 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, conditioning. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Plank (forearm / low plank) + Dead Bugs (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Seated Cable Row (bilateral) + Leg Press (machine) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Single-Arm Lat Pulldown + Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) + Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): finisher: Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top, Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time, Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down)_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**

   - Swap for: Medball Plank Rockers (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Plank (straight-arm plank) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way) / Hollow Rocks (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Plank%20(forearm%20%2F%20low%20plank)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Plank (forearm / low plank)`
2. **Dead Bugs** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Bird Dogs (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Cross Connect (rolls / rocking / hold) (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / Plank with Alternating Feet Raises (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / March (anti-rotation marching) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dead%20Bugs) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dead Bugs`

**Test**
1. **Test: 10-30 m sprint** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
2. **Test: 5-10-5 Pro-Agility Shuttle** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 2-3 timed runs with full rest, record the best.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=5-10-5%20Pro-Agility%20Shuttle)
3. **Test: countermovement jump** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 3 attempts, record the best.
   - Baseline test this week: it sets your starting numbers.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L)** — 3 × 6 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rotational%20Medball%20Chest%20Pass%20(R%20%26%20L)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Power Clean & Jerk (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hex-Bar Jump Squat (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Power Clean`

**Strength**
1. **Seated Cable Row (bilateral)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Chest-Supported Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / TRX Row (suspension row) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / Inverted Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the chest support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20Cable%20Row%20(bilateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated Cable Row (bilateral)`
2. **Leg Press (machine)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   > Constrained, no axial (spinal) load: a good second squat-pattern lift of the week.
   - Swap for: Pendulum Squat (machine) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Hack Squat (machine) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Double-KB Front-Rack Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the machine's support) / Goblet Squat (KB/DB) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Press%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Press (machine)`
3. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Week 1: pick a weight you could lift about 3 more times
   - Progress: when you hit 15 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - per side = 1 set

   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.

   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

## Week 2 — Getting used to it (small steps)

- +1 set on Front Squat (barbell) (Monday)

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_Average time: about 100-120 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, conditioning. You can also add or remove exercises._

_Superset to save time: Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Chin-Up (supinated) + Side (Lateral) Lunge (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Concentration Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): finisher: Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski) · extra jump / throw stations: Lateral Shuffles, Medball Chest Pass, Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine) · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Turkish Get-Ups (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Kettlebell Windmills - kneeling or standing (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Press (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Acceleration Starts** — 3 × 5 x 10 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Falling-Start Accelerations (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Acceleration%20Starts) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Acceleration Starts`
2. **Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing)** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Jump, then land and stick it: hold the landing 2-3 s, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Depth Drop (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Box%20Jump%20(sideways%20onto%20a%20low%20box%2C%20stick%20the%20landing)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing)`
3. **Medball Chest Pass** — 3 × 9 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Overhead Throw (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Chest%20Pass) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Chest Pass`
4. **Lateral Shuffles** — 3 × 9 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Slant Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Flat Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Dig / Out Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lateral%20Shuffles) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lateral Shuffles`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean & Jerk** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hex-Bar Jump Squat (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Power Clean & Jerk`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the heavier barbell load) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20Barbell%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 4 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Low-Bar Back Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High-Bar Back Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Chin-Up (supinated)** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Chin-Up%20(supinated)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Chin-Up (supinated)`
4. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`

**Accessories**
1. **Dumbbell Concentration Curl** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Cross-Body Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Arm DB Preacher Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Concentration%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dumbbell Concentration Curl`
2. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
3. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

**Conditioning**
1. **Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski)** — 1 × 20 cal · all-out sprint
   - Buy-out: a short all-out sprint to finish the session. With more than one bout, rest until you can go all-out again.
   - Swap for: Calorie Effort: 15 cal (bike / rower / ski) (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Calorie Effort: 10 cal (bike / rower / ski) (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Fast Run / Walk Back ('NOT a sprint') (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Short Fast Run / Sprint Buy-out (10-30 s) (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Calorie%20Effort%3A%2020%20cal%20(bike%20%2F%20rower%20%2F%20ski)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski)`

### Tuesday · B1 - hip hinge + push
_Average time: about 110-135 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Bird Dogs (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Barbell Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension + Nordic Hamstring Curl (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: DB Lateral (Side) Raise + Machine Calf Raise (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Flat Route, Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, DB Lateral (Side) Raise, Nordic Hamstring Curl, Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension_

4. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest

### Thursday · A2 - leg press / belt squat + pull
_Average time: about 90-110 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, conditioning. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Plank (forearm / low plank) + Dead Bugs (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) + Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L) (about 4.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Seated Cable Row (bilateral) + Leg Press (machine) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Single-Arm Lat Pulldown + Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) + Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): finisher: Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski) · extra jump / throw stations: Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L), Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top, Leg Extension (machine) - one leg at a time, Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down)_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Medball Plank Rockers (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Plank (straight-arm plank) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way) / Hollow Rocks (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Plank%20(forearm%20%2F%20low%20plank)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Plank (forearm / low plank)`
2. **Dead Bugs** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Bird Dogs (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Cross Connect (rolls / rocking / hold) (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / Plank with Alternating Feet Raises (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / March (anti-rotation marching) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dead%20Bugs) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dead Bugs`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Falling-Start Accelerations** — 3 × 5 x 10 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Acceleration Starts (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mach Sprint Drills (A-March / A-Skip / A-Run) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Falling-Start%20Accelerations) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Falling-Start Accelerations`
2. **Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Jump, then land and stick it: hold the landing 2-3 s, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Depth Drop (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Jump%20(side%20to%20side%20over%20a%20line%20or%20a%20low%20hurdle)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle)`
3. **Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L)** — 3 × 6 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Alternating Back-to-Wall Rotational Scoops (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rotational%20Medball%20Chest%20Pass%20(R%20%26%20L)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Rotational Medball Chest Pass (R & L)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Power Clean & Jerk (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hex-Bar Jump Squat (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Power Clean`

**Strength**
1. **Seated Cable Row (bilateral)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Chest-Supported Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / TRX Row (suspension row) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / Inverted Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the chest support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20Cable%20Row%20(bilateral)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated Cable Row (bilateral)`
2. **Leg Press (machine)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   > Constrained, no axial (spinal) load: a good second squat-pattern lift of the week.
   - Swap for: Pendulum Squat (machine) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Hack Squat (machine) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Double-KB Front-Rack Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the machine's support) / Goblet Squat (KB/DB) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Press%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Press (machine)`
3. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Close Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Lat%20Pulldown) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm Lat Pulldown`
4. **Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)** — 2 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Rear-Foot-Elevated%20Lunge%20(RFE%20%2F%20Bulgarian%20split%20squat)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat)`

**Accessories**

   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.

   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.

## Week 3 — Driving it

_Week level: 3.2 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**What changes this week**
- +1 set on Stiff-Leg Deadlift / RDL (Friday): 3 → 4 sets

**Athletic work this week:** 75 jump contacts · 58 ankle-hop contacts · 69 throws · 84 direction changes · 300 m of sprints · 9 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 29% · Receive-Create 1: Land

### Monday · A1 - squat + pull
_Average time: about 100-125 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, conditioning. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist) + Copenhagen Plank (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) + Medball Chest Pass (about 4.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip) + Front Squat (barbell) (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Chin-Up (supinated) + Side (Lateral) Lunge (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Dumbbell Concentration Curl + Leg Extension (machine) (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): finisher: Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski) · extra jump / throw stations: Lateral Shuffles, Medball Chest Pass, Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional), Leg Extension (machine) · 1 more_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=A-March%20(running%20drill)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `A-March (running drill)`

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**
1. **Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Russian Twist (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Cable Woodchopper (cable chop) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Turkish Get-Ups (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Kettlebell Windmills - kneeling or standing (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Cable%20Oblique%20Rotation%20(cable%20rotational%20twist)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Cable Oblique Rotation (cable rotational twist)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 20-40 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Press (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Acceleration Starts** — 3 × 5 x 10 m · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Falling-Start Accelerations (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Max-Velocity Sprint (Top Speed) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Acceleration%20Starts) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Acceleration Starts`
2. **Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing)** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Jump, then land and stick it: hold the landing 2-3 s, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Depth Drop (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Two-Foot%20Lateral%20Box%20Jump%20(sideways%20onto%20a%20low%20box%2C%20stick%20the%20landing)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing)`
3. **Medball Chest Pass** — 3 × 9 Reps · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Overhead Throw (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Chest%20Pass) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Chest Pass`
4. **Lateral Shuffles** — 3 × 10 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Slant Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Flat Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Dig / Out Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lateral%20Shuffles) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lateral Shuffles`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Clean & Jerk** — 3 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Power Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Low-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hex-Bar Jump Squat (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Clean%20%26%20Jerk) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Power Clean & Jerk`

**Strength**
1. **Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the heavier barbell load) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20Barbell%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - Barbell (Supinated Grip)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 4 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Low-Bar Back Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High-Bar Back Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / Zercher Squat (barbell) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Chin-Up (supinated)** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Strict Pull-Up (Knees to Chest / tuck) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Pull-up (keeps the vertical pull; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Chin-Up%20(supinated)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Chin-Up (supinated)`
4. **Side (Lateral) Lunge** — 2 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Hold dumbbells at your sides (or a barbell on your back) once your body weight is easy at this effort
   - Swap for: Step-Up (forward and lateral) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Stationary Lunge (Split Squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20(Lateral)%20Lunge) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side (Lateral) Lunge`

**Accessories**
1. **Dumbbell Concentration Curl** — 2 × 12 to 15 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Cross-Body Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Arm DB Preacher Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dumbbell%20Concentration%20Curl) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dumbbell Concentration Curl`
2. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
3. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

**Conditioning**
1. **Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski)** — 1 × 20 cal · all-out sprint
   - Buy-out: a short all-out sprint to finish the session. With more than one bout, rest until you can go all-out again.
   - Swap for: Calorie Effort: 15 cal (bike / rower / ski) (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Calorie Effort: 10 cal (bike / rower / ski) (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Fast Run / Walk Back ('NOT a sprint') (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Short Fast Run / Sprint Buy-out (10-30 s) (keeps the conditioning effect; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Calorie%20Effort%3A%2020%20cal%20(bike%20%2F%20rower%20%2F%20ski)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Calorie Effort: 20 cal (bike / rower / ski)`

### Tuesday · B1 - hip hinge + push
_Average time: about 110-135 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories, cardio. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Bird Dogs (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Barbell Bench Press + 45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension) (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down) + Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension + Nordic Hamstring Curl (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: DB Lateral (Side) Raise + Machine Calf Raise (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Flat Route, Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, DB Lateral (Side) Raise, Nordic Hamstring Curl, Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. **A-March (running drill)**
   - Swap for: A-Skip (running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / A-Runs (high-knee running drill) (keeps the sprint work; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)

**Core (optional: you may switch it off or change it)**

   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Press (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / High (Straight-Arm) Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Lateral Crunch (keeps the core work; loses the held position)

   - per side = 1 set

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height)** — 3 × 6 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - Jump, then land and stick it: hold the landing 2-3 s, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Two-Foot Lateral Box Jump (sideways onto a low box, stick the landing) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Two-Foot Lateral Jump (side to side over a line or a low hurdle) (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Low Depth Drop (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Mid Box Jump (Knee Height) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)

2. **Ankle Hops (bilateral, in place)** — 2 × 14 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - Swap for: Bilateral Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Lateral Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the same direction of movement) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Zig-Zag Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)

3. **Flat Route** — 3 × 9 cuts · max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Swap for: Lateral Shuffles (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Slant Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Post / Corner Route (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / L-Test / L-Drill (3-Cone) (keeps the change of direction; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20Route) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat Route`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Power Snatch** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Snatch Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Single-Arm DB Snatch (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Mid Hang (knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Low Hang (below the knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Power%20Snatch) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Power Snatch`

**Strength**
1. **Barbell Bench Press** — 4 × 8-10 Reps · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Flat DB Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the heavier barbell load) / Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work)

   - Swap for: GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
3. **Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)** — 3 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / Barbell Z-Press (seated on floor) (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / Incline DB Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / DB Z-Press (seated on floor) (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Alternating%20Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press%20(one%20up%20%2F%20one%20down)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Alternating Incline DB Bench Press (one up / one down)`
4. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 2 × 10 to 12 per side · leave 3 in the tank (3 RIR, RPE 7) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 per side on every set with 3 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
